<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/** Parse "YYYY-MM-DD" (or any ISO string) to UTC midnight ms. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> utcDay</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">iso</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">y</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">m</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> iso.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">slice</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">split</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"-"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">map</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(Number)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Date.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">UTC</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(y, (m </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, d </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> isoDay</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ms</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/** Inclusive range of UTC dates ("YYYY-MM-DD"); `end` is absent while a range is being picked. */</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> HeatCalendarProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Omit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ComponentProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"div"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"children"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> readonly</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> HeatCalendarDatum</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Last UTC day shown ("YYYY-MM-DD"); defaults to the latest date in `data`. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  endDate</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  weeks</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** 0 = Sunday (contribution-graph convention), 1 = Monday. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  weekStartsOn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Noun after every count. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  unit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** The single hue; strength maps to magnitude. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  color</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span></span>
